#### Interpretation and alternatives

**CL-B0250-001 — The important day changes scale**

The vignette first makes a conventionally large two-year milestone the answer to the others' uncertainty, then frustrates a second expectation with the arbitrary-looking letter number392678. Nicole's answer is relational rather than numerological: an individual letter, a child's wave and a shared meal can all authorize attention. Rui translates this into the value of ordinary time and a past that need not be dismissed. The title's732nd-day form participates in this pressure on which counts deserve commemoration; it does not require the analyst to force a calendar or resolve exact day arithmetic.

Mito's preference for gratitude through song encounters Nicole's request for plain words, and the request actually obtains a concise direct thanks. Yet Nicole later supports withholding live/album particulars, with Mito and Mana giving their own reasons. The objects of disclosure differ: acknowledging an existing relation can require explicit speech while preserving anticipation of a work. This selective contrast is better supported than either a general cult of silence or a claim that everyone now believes words always suffice.

### Complete-family factual reconstruction

- **Pages:** P0-26

**Account:** The complete single-document family opens with Nicole/Mito preparing a party and disagreeing over decoration and okara-doughnut condiments. Mana, Memoru, Rui and Kurt enter with their own questions, food interests and appraisals. Rui asks the occasion and explicitly objects after Nicole's first forehead flick. The other members' uncertainty is part of the scene.
- **Pages:** P27-49

**Account:** Nicole identifies the person beyond the screen as the second-anniversary addressee. Mito confirms the intended thanks and moves from proposing song to an actual plain thanks after Nicole asks. Mana and Rui give their own acknowledgments; Kurt anticipates more shared work, and Memoru mixes a support appeal with a wallet joke answered by a jointly labelled peer response. Nicole stops Kurt's almost-disclosure of a next live and album; Mito and Mana favor their own means of conveying future work without giving particulars.
- **Pages:** P50-75

**Account:** A second flick and Rui's complaint lead into another occasion. Mito explicitly does not know it despite having helped prepare. Nicole names fan letter392678; the others question its unroundness. Nicole gives value to every letter, reports yesterday's child's wave and the previous-day ramen outing with Mana/Kurt, and proposes every day as an anniversary. Kurt affirms the meal's pleasure; Memoru, Rui, Mana and Mito each adapt or qualify the idea in different registers.
- **Pages:** P76-85

**Account:** Nicole invites a lively coming year with the audience and borrows GUTS, prompting Memoru's objection. Fade commands then give Nicole a centered coda: repeated thanks, affection, a self-voiced question about already being understood and a renewed invitation/farewell. The greeting closes; no actual future live/album or independently speaking audience response is supplied.

### Complete-family causal structure

- The initial preparation establishes a shared activity but not shared information: Mito knows the anniversary purpose, while later questions expose the others' partial knowledge and Mito's ignorance of the second occasion. Nicole's sequence of disclosures gives the conversation its forward movement.
- Explicit gratitude and selective future-work secrecy are different communicative tasks. Nicole's request elicits Mito's concise thanks; Nicole's later interruption prevents Kurt from giving forthcoming particulars, with Mito/Mana supplying their own reasons. The text supports this local distinction without a general ban on speech or a doctrine of complete transparency.
- The unround fan-letter count produces a challenge to what merits commemoration. Nicole answers with the value of any letter and particular recent contacts, allowing Rui to articulate the significance of ordinary time and the past. Each uptake remains an utterance, not proof that all prior harm was necessary, forgiven or morally justified.
- The final group invitation is interrupted by a style/catchphrase objection, and the fade creates a further private-looking audience address. Repetition of gratitude closes the document's address while leaving its promised future beyond the attained horizon. Catalog order supplies no cause or exact calendar relation to the unopened LeSca/4U families.

### Complete-family epistemic boundaries

- Nicole explicitly states the second anniversary and later the fan-letter ordinal; both are source-local claims. A title reading732日目のメッセージ does not identify an absolute date/year or prove a two-year elapsed Main timeline. The screen-address frame must remain visible rather than being naturalized into a dated six-member Main reunion.
- Mito's preparation knowledge is limited: she explains the first purpose atP29and denies any other knowledge atP59. Mana/Rui/Memoru/Kurt learn or question information as staged. Their inquiries and Nicole's disclosures must not be filled retrospectively from a later family or release schedule.
- The child's wave and meal occur as Nicole's reports of yesterday and the day before yesterday; Kurt confirms the latter's deliciousness. There is no new on-scene child body, external food test, independently counted fan-mail archive or new stable identity for the reported girl.
- The coda's apparent audience reply exists only through Nicole's own question and uptake. No separate Player/audience turn, choice/input field, agreement, payment, attendance or romantic reciprocity is evidenced. Source address can invite response without establishing it.
- Joint speaker labels remain joint written carriers; individual native IsSpeaker/IsFront flags are retained even when they differ from a naive one-speaker-per-slot model. No independently heard quartet/duet or acoustic silence has been inferred.

### Complete-family character relations

- Rona→Kyoko: de-escalation request, initially written offscreen. Rona→Shizuka: asks her to stop joking and help. Honoka→Kyoko: asks for calm. - Susu→Kyoko: proposes joint practice, then compares her to Musubi and calls her anger cute. Kyoko→Susu: repeated refusal and objection to being deflected by praise. Neither acceptance at this point nor reciprocal romance is established. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0201.json__2abe9dd6e46c444b?doc=primary&log=34#/Pages/34` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0201.json__2abe9dd6e46c444b?doc=primary&log=42#/Pages/42` (complete P34-42 context).

- Rena→peers: interprets Kyoko's responsibility. Rona→peers: cites gifts/visits. Honoka→Rona/peers: requests secrecy and quotes Kyoko's overpurchase account. Kyoko→Rona/Honoka/all: threatens lectures and asks deletion of embarrassing knowledge. The recipients have already heard it; compliance is not memory erasure. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0201.json__2abe9dd6e46c444b?doc=primary&log=43#/Pages/43` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0201.json__2abe9dd6e46c444b?doc=primary&log=55#/Pages/55` (complete P43-55 context).

Record this completed local concession before the contest. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0201.json__2abe9dd6e46c444b?doc=primary&log=56#/Pages/56` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0201.json__2abe9dd6e46c444b?doc=primary&log=65#/Pages/65` (complete P56-65 context).

- Susu→peers: moves from an ellipsis to a proposed time-stake contest, leaves a non-contest option, and boasts. Kyoko→Susu: takes the challenge. Rona questions, Rena is bemused, Honoka lacks confidence. Do not turn these different responses into unanimous prior assent or proven access to Susu's private motive. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0201.json__2abe9dd6e46c444b?doc=primary&log=59#/Pages/59`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0201.json__2abe9dd6e46c444b?doc=primary&log=66#/Pages/66` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0201.json__2abe9dd6e46c444b?doc=primary&log=78#/Pages/78` (complete P66-78 context).

#### Questions and limits at this original exit

- The chapter ends before the first7thTwistersround. What actual instructions, choices, positions, results and allocation, if any, does the unopened successor establish? Preserve the accepted joint-practice concession and later challenge as separate prior steps.
- Susu's private motive and the meaning of her written ellipsis are not directly supplied. Does later action clarify the proposed contest without retroactively making every first-chapter response knowing or unanimous?
- Which booking records, Player meeting details or alternative practice times actually exist remains unverified here. No later convenience can make Kyoko's current guess an already established fact.
- The formal/durable status of the temporary named four-person side, exact calendar/major-live occurrence, technical performance, wider biographical and readiness/monograph obligations remain open;2053unopened.

No booking ledger, actual meeting, fee, disciplinary fault or universal lack of alternatives is independently inspected.
- Kyoko's first-time comparison concerns the major-live context at issue; it does not establish that LeSca has never performed anything. Her777/4U/KARAKURI appraisals preserve speaker and context, without objective ranking, new unit chronology or technical performance review.
- Rona's gift account, Honoka's quoted overpurchase explanation, Rena's motive reading and Shizuka's grandfather analogy remain separately attributed. Kyoko's delete/forget demand is a social request, not actual erased memory; her promised lecture is not completed.
- Kyoko actually accepts joint practice atP64before Susu proposes a contest atP69. Susu offers an alternative and boasts; no hidden perfect plan, unanimous assent, lawful wager or permanent fair-governance procedure is supplied.
- The seven-person scene does not become a seven-member777lineup. The temporary four-person スシ☆ロ☆スイ comparison name is not a new biological identity, global registry record, durable musical group or established new UNIT until separately reviewed integration.

#### Interpretation and alternatives

**CL-B0250-021 — A contest whose stated rule and attained result diverge**

7thTwisters yields a reported draw, the tea contest yields compliments without adjudication, and dancing continues precisely because judging is unclear. Repetition gradually makes shared activity more consequential than defeating the other side. Susu's later stated wish to dance supplies a supported local motive for the final proposal; it does not establish a flawless exclusive master plan behind every earlier action.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0202.json__62c45248ced97f97?doc=primary&log=1#/Pages/1` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0202.json__62c45248ced97f97?doc=primary&log=38#/Pages/38` (complete P1-38 context); `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0202.json__62c45248ced97f97?doc=primary&log=83#/Pages/83` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0202.json__62c45248ced97f97?doc=primary&log=109#/Pages/109` (complete P83-109 context).

**CL-B0250-022 — Kyoko's revised practical judgment**

Kyoko continues to protect scarce rehearsal time, yet accepts the dance, recognizes the shared practice, admits enjoyment and invites its continuation. This develops her earlier conditional agreement in the first chapter rather than pretending she had never consented to joint practice at all. The final competitive ambition survives the concession, so cooperation modifies rather than abolishes rivalry.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0202.json__62c45248ced97f97?doc=primary&log=32#/Pages/32`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0202.json__62c45248ced97f97?doc=primary&log=37#/Pages/37`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0202.json__62c45248ced97f97?doc=primary&log=44#/Pages/44`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0202.json__62c45248ced97f97?doc=primary&log=78#/Pages/78`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0202.json__62c45248ced97f97?doc=primary&log=81#/Pages/81`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0202.json__62c45248ced97f97?doc=primary&log=87#/Pages/87`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0202.json__62c45248ced97f97?doc=primary&log=105#/Pages/105` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0202.json__62c45248ced97f97?doc=primary&log=115#/Pages/115` (complete P105-115 context).

**CL-B0250-023 — Concrete reception rather than unconditional uplift**

Exhaustion, uncertain criteria, fun, technical compliments and reduced anxiety are separately attributed reports. Honoka's 'somewhat' and Kyoko's agreement support a bounded easing in this encounter, not a cure or a verified future live result. Kyoko's affirmative invitation is stronger evidence of changed willingness than the analyst merely reading a smiling still.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0202.json__62c45248ced97f97?doc=primary&log=98#/Pages/98` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0202.json__62c45248ced97f97?doc=primary&log=114#/Pages/114` (complete P98-114 context).

**CL-B0250-024 — Comedic intrusion remains an objection-bearing event**

Rena's teasing, Kyoko's request not to be stared at, Kyoko's startled objection, Honoka's cautions, Rena's warning about joining and Sui's request not to cling remain distinct responses. The favorable later practice does not retrospectively license every earlier physical or verbal advance. The P17-20 adjacency supports an intrusive gesture inference, with exact contact and anatomy unresolved by the native packet and static portraits.

**CL-B0250-025 — Care and competence distributed across members**

Shizuka's and Honoka's tea, Kyoko's endorsement of Honoka, Rona's doughnut offer, Honoka's offer to help, and Sui's question about the name give the ensemble different practical and interpretive contributions. Honoka's self-minimizing speech is answered by situated appreciation without requiring an objective body ranking, universal mastery or analyst diagnosis.

**CL-B0250-026 — Susu's mediation is neither pure manipulation nor universal consent**

Playful wishes, flirtatious hypothetical address, a tea proposal, a shopping request and the final dance coexist. The final stated wish and Rona's report explain an interest in LeSca; they do not erase Kyoko's feeling of being set up or establish that everyone knowingly agreed to all the means. ### Complete-family factual reconstruction

- **Pages:** 507020101P0-33

**Account:** Rena wins the narrated run with Sui, then the seven discover a contested practice slot. Kyoko insists LeSca needs its booked rehearsal; Honoka reports the Player is away at an important live meeting. The other participants recount their encounter and arrival. Kyoko conjectures a management mistake, but no reservation ledger or observed Player action establishes fault.
- **Pages:** 507020101P34-65

**Account:** Kyoko articulates concern about the scale of LeSca's coming live and leads her side. Rena, Honoka and the visitors supply their own appraisals, including reports of Rona's care and a grandfather/club-captain analogy. Shizuka offers rescheduling, Rona reports other slots occupied, and Honoka proposes joint practice. Rena's unfinished leader-capacity prompt is followed by Kyoko's explicit conditional agreement if her two bandmates accept.
- **Pages:** 507020101P66-105

**Account:** Susu proposes a time-stake contest, retains shared practice as an option and boasts; Kyoko accepts. Shizuka theatrically names the four-person side スシ☆ロ☆スイ, with Sui tentatively interpreting the component names. Strength play and renewed Rena/Sui competition lead Shizuka to supply7thTwisters. Rena chooses the first matchup and Sui accepts; this chapter ends before a result.
- **Pages:** 507020102P0-38

**Account:** Rona directs the game while Rena teases Sui, Honoka objects, Susu imagines other play and an intrusive-seeming gesture prompts Kyoko's protest. Susu's wish to join prompts explicit danger/proximity objections. After transition, Sui reports a draw caused by Susu barging in, and Rena agrees to a later rematch. Kyoko continues to stress practice time.
- **Pages:** 507020102P39-80

**Account:** Shizuka and Honoka's tea becomes the next proposed contest. In the lounge, each offers a serving and receives particular praise/thanks; no winner is named. Rona offers practice doughnuts and Honoka offers help. Sui asks the group's name; Honoka expands it as lemon squash and Rena supplies their youthful naming rationale. Susu's buying request momentarily distracts Kyoko before she recalls the contest.
- **Pages:** 507020102P81-109

**Account:** Back in the studio, Susu proposes dance using a shared but unnamed song. Both sides agree. Rona's unfinished recognition is hushed. After elision, the participants report prolonged dancing without clear judging criteria, exchange positive appraisals and call it mutual learning. Kyoko recognizes shared practice and feels set up. Susu says she wanted to dance with LeSca; Rona reports her earlier liking for their songs.
- **Pages:** 507020102P110-122

**Account:** Honoka reports somewhat less live anxiety and Kyoko agrees. Kyoko explicitly says she enjoyed the experience and invites continued joint practice; Sui accepts and Rena rallies them. Kyoko still vows LeSca will stand out most, prompting affectionate/teasing reactions and her objection to premature familiarity. The end subtitle closes the vignette before any future live result.

### Complete-family causal structure

- The double-booking problem generates a genuine resource conflict, but its agent and administrative cause remain unverified. Honoka's joint-practice proposal and Rena's unfinished prompt already elicit Kyoko's conditional agreement before the games. The family must not falsely simplify its trajectory into unbroken refusal until Susu alone saves it.
- The subsequent time-stake challenge makes rivalry a vehicle for activity rather than resolving the booking by authoritative allocation.7thTwisters produces a reported draw, tea produces appreciation without scoring, and dance proceeds with criteria so unclear that participants keep going. Contest form progressively carries the very shared practice it purportedly postpones.
- Kyoko's time complaints remain intelligible counterpressure. Tea is received as calming by others and later dancing is received as useful practice; these differing appraisals explain how the same session can feel like distraction and become valuable without proving either every minute wasted or all complaints insincere.
- Susu's explicit wish to dance and Rona's earlier-song-preference report become disclosed only in the conclusion. They support a local account of her interest in the final activity. Hypothetical games, flirtatious address and intrusive-seeming boundary testing coexist, so the ending does not establish a perfectly benevolent, fully prearranged or universally understood master plan.
- Reciprocal performance compliments and Honoka's partial anxiety reduction precede Kyoko's admission of enjoyment and active invitation. This positive change is explicitly enacted in words and acceptance, rather than inferred from the apparent warmth of the ending alone.
- LeSca's ambition survives successful cooperation. Kyoko's final claim to stand out, Honoka's complaint about spoiling harmony and the ensuing wordplay retain the group's differences while permitting continued practice. No winner, permanent merger or future concert triumph is required for this local resolution.

### Complete-family character relations

- Kyoko combines rehearsal responsibility, boundaries and competitive pride with genuine concessions. Her earlier conditional agreement, praise of Honoka, acceptance of dancing and final active invitation describe a situated change without converting her into a permanently cured obstacle or declaring her discomfort false.
- Honoka supplies the early shared-practice proposal, mediates uncertainty, voices cautions, serves tea and offers help. Her later reduced anxiety is explicitly partial; competence and being encouraged coexist with self-deprecation rather than erasing it.
- Rena's athletic teasing and Sui rivalry persist alongside an explicit safety warning, appreciation of tea, reciprocal performance praise and LeSca team encouragement. Neither proximity banter nor common activity proves universal consent or romance.
- Susu supplies multiple proposals and eventually states a desire to dance with LeSca. Her warmth and effective mediation coexist with others' irritation and intrusive-seeming boundary crossings. The analyst must retain both instead of flattening her into pure manipulator or faultless healer.
- Rona directs play, offers practical care, praises LeSca and reports a relevant earlier preference. Her hesitant unfinished recognition remains incomplete, and current Rona qualifications prevent reducing her to generic helplessness.
- Shizuka's theatrical ceremony, stated tea/archery skills, polite exchange, mutual-learning appraisal and pun are distinct contributions. Grandfather analogies and her public aspiration are situated speech, not proof of objective historical doctrine.
- Sui participates as a rival, expresses bodily/proximity objections, reports the draw, asks about the name and accepts renewed practice. The seven-person collaboration does not dissolve LeSca/777/4U boundaries or promote any READY/monograph status.

### Complete-family literary synthesis and rivals

**CL-B0250-030 — The first concession precedes the games**

Honoka proposes joint practice and Rena's unfinished leader-capacity prompt precedes Kyoko's conditional agreement if both bandmates are content. Susu's later contest is therefore not the sole origin of collaboration. The final invitation is a further affirmative development from an already conceded possibility, while the booking fault remains unverified.

#### Interpretation and alternatives

**CL-B0250-034 — A surprise whose information is unevenly held**

Hina and Emoco initially deny knowing Ume's immediate purpose, while Shinju knows the day-off information and Emoco later discloses the future live arrangement. These are different objects of knowledge: ignorance of why Ume summoned them does not imply ignorance of all4Uplans. The staged delay, attempted cover story and final interruption create the chapter's forward movement without requiring universal foreknowledge.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0203.json__09c67be4005985cd?doc=primary&log=3#/Pages/3` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0203.json__09c67be4005985cd?doc=primary&log=11#/Pages/11` (complete P3-11 context); `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0203.json__09c67be4005985cd?doc=primary&log=51#/Pages/51` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0203.json__09c67be4005985cd?doc=primary&log=68#/Pages/68` (complete P51-68 context); `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0203.json__09c67be4005985cd?doc=primary&log=102#/Pages/102` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0203.json__09c67be4005985cd?doc=primary&log=117#/Pages/117` (complete P102-117 context).

**CL-B0250-035 — Rivalry as a claimed performance and contested hierarchy**

Ume explicitly calls her manner a rival presentation while denying idols rival status. The inconsistent rhetoric seeks a desired reaction and status, and the others repeatedly respond differently. Emoco's mock-policing is a speech act, not an actual police report; the contradiction is local evidence rather than an excuse to erase Ume's prior convictions or declare all antagonism affectionate.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0203.json__09c67be4005985cd?doc=primary&log=21#/Pages/21` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0203.json__09c67be4005985cd?doc=primary&log=38#/Pages/38` (complete P21-38 context); `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0203.json__09c67be4005985cd?doc=primary&log=94#/Pages/94` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0203.json__09c67be4005985cd?doc=primary&log=104#/Pages/104` (complete P94-104 context).

**CL-B0250-036 — Care can be difficult to receive**

Sumire offers practical help and invites Shinju to depend on her more; Shinju's promise to try turns receiving care into an effort rather than simple refusal. Later hosting permits continued conversation without requiring Ume to confess her desired visit. These concrete accommodations do not establish actual universal equality or erase Sumire's remembered bad experience.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0203.json__09c67be4005985cd?doc=primary&log=14#/Pages/14` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0203.json__09c67be4005985cd?doc=primary&log=18#/Pages/18` (complete P14-18 context); `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0203.json__09c67be4005985cd?doc=primary&log=69#/Pages/69` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0203.json__09c67be4005985cd?doc=primary&log=73#/Pages/73` (complete P69-73 context); `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0203.json__09c67be4005985cd?doc=primary&log=83#/Pages/83` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0203.json__09c67be4005985cd?doc=primary&log=88#/Pages/88` (complete P83-88 context).

**CL-B0250-037 — Agreement between Ume and Emoco has a cost to Hina**

Hina tries to stop the quarrel and calls the two close. Their ensuing cheek-poking cooperation answers that claim comically, but Hina explicitly asks them to stop and questions Ume's participation. Sumire's friendly-looking appraisal must be attributed to her; it cannot overwrite Hina's dissent or serve as analyst proof of consent, harmlessness or absence of prior serious conflict.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0203.json__09c67be4005985cd?doc=primary&log=35#/Pages/35` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0203.json__09c67be4005985cd?doc=primary&log=50#/Pages/50` (complete P35-50 context).

**CL-B0250-038 — An explanation actively challenged rather than silently accepted**

Shinju's day-off recollection, Hina's tutoring report and Emoco's cancelled-plan report put pressure on Ume's accidental work explanation. Ume's admission and request for them to play along support reading the new-song account as a locally improvised cover, while the actual existence of a new composition is not established. Shinju later lets the account stand provisionally to elicit the real message, which differs from endorsing its truth.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0203.json__09c67be4005985cd?doc=primary&log=55#/Pages/55` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0203.json__09c67be4005985cd?doc=primary&log=68#/Pages/68` (complete P55-68 context); `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0203.json__09c67be4005985cd?doc=primary&log=80#/Pages/80` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0203.json__09c67be4005985cd?doc=primary&log=95#/Pages/95` (complete P80-95 context).

**CL-B0250-039 — Hospitality with a remembered history**

Sumire's tea offer explicitly recalls the earlier visit and complaint. Emoco's jab and Sumire's reply keep the memory's unpleasantness active, while Kajika marks her own prior absence. Hina's pleasure in sweets does not make every4Umember accept the same social meaning, and the handmade question is not itself confirmation of who baked them.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0203.json__09c67be4005985cd?doc=primary&log=83#/Pages/83` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0203.json__09c67be4005985cd?doc=primary&log=89#/Pages/89` (complete P83-89 context).

**CL-B0250-040 — Reported permission changes the meaning of an invasion claim**

Ume withholds a reason and frames a looming major event; Emoco names the planned4Uappearance and reports prior notice plus enthusiastic management welcome. This qualifies a naive reading of necessarily unauthorized disruption. The managers' actual conversation is not supplied, so reported approval and Haru's explicit present welcome are retained as different evidence, with the future appearance still prospective.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0203.json__09c67be4005985cd?doc=primary&log=109#/Pages/109` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0203.json__09c67be4005985cd?doc=primary&log=118#/Pages/118` (complete P109-118 context).

**CL-B0250-041 — Haru's invitation and Ume's disrupted script**

Haru receives Ume's knowledge with thanks and the announced appearance with delight, while Ume repeatedly asks for a different uptake. Haru's expressed welcome is positive local evidence, not proof Ume's every denial is false affection or that the group has forgiven all earlier harm. Sumire's final agreement aligns her with the feeling of conversational disorientation rather than an independently established judgment about Haru's motives.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0203.json__09c67be4005985cd?doc=primary&log=78#/Pages/78` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0203.json__09c67be4005985cd?doc=primary&log=82#/Pages/82` (complete P78-82 context); `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0203.json__09c67be4005985cd?doc=primary&log=102#/Pages/102` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0203.json__09c67be4005985cd?doc=primary&log=120#/Pages/120` (complete P102-120 context).

**CL-B0250-042 — New live hopes retain their prospective force**

August, a larger repertoire, difficult new choreography and Haru's hoped-for better stage are explicit source-local particulars. The chapter does not give an exact year, name a newly admitted song, show the practice or fulfill the future performance. Catalog placement after summer LeSca and anniversary material supplies no additional calendar equation.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0203.json__09c67be4005985cd?doc=primary&log=102#/Pages/102` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0203.json__09c67be4005985cd?doc=primary&log=111#/Pages/111` (complete P102-111 context).

**CL-B0250-043 — Literal names and qualified current states**

The abbreviated はる☆ジカ mention must be read with the current full はる☆ジカ（ちいさな）/UNIT0033qualifier and Shinju's unfinished attempt to correct it. Emoco's displaced 何咲コニル label may suggest Coney in management context, but the literal is retained and no new person or definitive normalized identity is invented. Current published context guides the analyst without becoming knowledge automatically possessed by every character here.

Ume's written ellipsis prompts Hina's question. Emoco speculates Ume regrets not exchanging addresses; Ume denies it. Hina proposes asking at the live, but Ume repeats that she is thinking of defeating Nanasta and making the best stage, then tells her bandmates to follow. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0204.json__57924fb0af8f298b?doc=primary&log=80#/Pages/80` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0204.json__57924fb0af8f298b?doc=primary&log=90#/Pages/90` (complete P80-90 context).

The leadership dispute, contact exchange and future live are not resolved by the ending. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0204.json__57924fb0af8f298b?doc=primary&log=91#/Pages/91` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0204.json__57924fb0af8f298b?doc=primary&log=108#/Pages/108` (complete P91-108 context).

#### Interpretation and alternatives

**CL-B0250-044 — Confidence that coexists with uncertainty**

Haru does not answer Ume with a settled definition of idol or a claim to have equalled every predecessor. She acknowledges4U's strength and the group's limits, then grounds confidence in the work and ongoing selves represented by their proposed stage. Kajika's admitted lack of ease and Sumire's difficulty reinforce confidence under pressure rather than a frictionless competence transformation.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0204.json__57924fb0af8f298b?doc=primary&log=22#/Pages/22` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0204.json__57924fb0af8f298b?doc=primary&log=32#/Pages/32` (complete P22-32 context).

**CL-B0250-045 — An explicit but conditional recognition**

Ume moves from predicting she will swallow the live to declaring these present Nanasta members worth rival competition, while retaining a general disclaimer about idols and imposing a performance demand. This is a supported local acknowledgment, not merely an analyst guessing affection beneath denial. Haru's shared-effort response makes rivalry compatible with participation without proving universal ideological conversion.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0204.json__57924fb0af8f298b?doc=primary&log=19#/Pages/19` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0204.json__57924fb0af8f298b?doc=primary&log=38#/Pages/38` (complete P19-38 context).

**CL-B0250-046 — The earlier absence remains a contested account**

Prior appearance and Ume's fever/absence gain several participants' acknowledgment, but excitement as medical cause is Emoco's taunt and Ume disputes it. The 知恵熱 sequence exposes disagreement about a word, not a diagnosis. Emoco's firing rhetoric does not enact dismissal or make the Ume-less lineup the canonical4Uformation.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0204.json__57924fb0af8f298b?doc=primary&log=4#/Pages/4` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0204.json__57924fb0af8f298b?doc=primary&log=18#/Pages/18` (complete P4-18 context).

**CL-B0250-047 — Independent ties break a simple two-camp model**

Hina knows the full unit name through Shinju, and their reported email/advice/family contact is news to Ume. This demonstrates a local information network not exhausted by band leadership or a rivalry script. Shinju's admission of not knowing why the qualifier matters keeps transmitted naming knowledge separate from knowledge of its rationale.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0204.json__57924fb0af8f298b?doc=primary&log=44#/Pages/44` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0204.json__57924fb0af8f298b?doc=primary&log=55#/Pages/55` (complete P44-55 context).

**CL-B0250-048 — Friendly invitation is not a completed exchange**

Haru asks rather than already possessing Ume's address; Ume neither provides it nor unequivocally accepts the request. Emoco's later regret speculation and Hina's reassurance suggest a possible path but remain their appraisals, with Ume's denial preserved. No mutual private contact, romance, exclusive bond or completed communication follows.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0204.json__57924fb0af8f298b?doc=primary&log=56#/Pages/56` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0204.json__57924fb0af8f298b?doc=primary&log=66#/Pages/66` (complete P56-66 context); `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0204.json__57924fb0af8f298b?doc=primary&log=83#/Pages/83` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0204.json__57924fb0af8f298b?doc=primary&log=89#/Pages/89` (complete P83-89 context).

**CL-B0250-049 — Different people receive the visit differently**

Nanasta's noisy-visitor appraisal, Haru's renewed resolve, Kajika's assent, Sumire's host pride and Shinju's time reminder remain distinct.4U's later coda gives Hina a positive appraisal and Emoco a qualified diversion account; it does not convert all seven into one unqualified emotional conclusion. The group rally is a written carrier rather than independently heard individual testimony.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0204.json__57924fb0af8f298b?doc=primary&log=70#/Pages/70` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0204.json__57924fb0af8f298b?doc=primary&log=88#/Pages/88` (complete P70-88 context).

**CL-B0250-050 — An outward recognition does not settle internal hierarchy**

Ume's order to follow becomes the occasion for Emoco to contest her leadership claim. Ume insists and Emoco rejects it, so the exchange is evidence of disputed authority rather than a completed election or authorized roster update. Their cooperation against Hina's cheeks again follows her attempt to stop a quarrel, preserving a recurring cost of mediation.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0204.json__57924fb0af8f298b?doc=primary&log=90#/Pages/90` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0204.json__57924fb0af8f298b?doc=primary&log=101#/Pages/101` (complete P90-101 context).

**CL-B0250-051 — The coda's repetition preserves dissent**

Hina's stop request, threats and actual next protest support a repeated cheek-poking episode, while the sound/shake commands structure a comic extension. Neither comedy, earlier warmth nor shared future ambitions provides consent or cancels historical4Uharm. Exact mechanics and performed tone remain outside the acquired modalities.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0204.json__57924fb0af8f298b?doc=primary&log=98#/Pages/98` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0204.json__57924fb0af8f298b?doc=primary&log=108#/Pages/108` (complete P98-108 context).

**CL-B0250-052 — A source-local future without forced calendar repair**

Ume says this year and the preceding chapter names August. Haru represents LeSca and her Kajika collaboration as upcoming-stage units. These are prospective particulars in this source frame; they do not complete the live, establish an absolute year or license rewriting the current earlier Christmas-limited/continuation-undecidedUNIT0033accounts into permanent status or a forced common timeline.

**CL-B0250-053 — Presentation articulates two receptions**

The office departure, Nanasta rally, black fill, warm-lit playground and later empty-office ending provide an ordered presentation contrast. The same source objects do not prove measured travel, a later real-world date, an actual return to the office, heard group unison or performed music. The narrative's two-sided coda is stronger than a generic resolved-friendship summary.

### Complete-family factual reconstruction

- **Pages:** 507020301P0-20

**Account:** Ume summons4Uto a park without telling Hina and Emoco her immediate purpose. Sumire and Shinju return from shopping and discuss accepting practical help despite rehearsal demands. Sumire abbreviates the Haru/Kajika unit name; Shinju starts to qualify it before Ume's reveal.
- **Pages:** 507020301P21-73

**Account:** Ume stages rival theatrics while denying idols rival standing, and Emoco repeatedly deflates it. Hina's attempted mediation is followed by Emoco/Ume cheek-poking and Hina's explicit requests to stop. Sumire appraises the display as friendly-looking. Shinju's day-off recollection and the bandmates' displaced-plan reports challenge Ume's work explanation; Ume admits the day off and asks them to play along. Sumire invites the trio to Nanasta, where Ume reluctantly agrees to go.
- **Pages:** 507020301P77-101

**Account:** Haru welcomes the surprising visit while Ume repeats it was accidental. Sumire serves tea with a memory of Emoco's earlier complaint; Kajika says she was absent then, and Hina praises sweets while asking if they are handmade. Shinju provisionally allows Ume's explanation to stand and asks for the message, advancing the conversation through further teasing.
- **Pages:** 507020301P102-121

**Account:** Ume names the large August live. Kajika reports difficult practice, Haru hopes their growth can yield a better stage and offers to inquire about invitation seats. Ume withholds why that is unnecessary; Emoco discloses4U's planned appearance, reports prior notification to the local manager and the source-displaced 何咲コニル, and quotes enthusiastic welcome. Haru explicitly welcomes sharing the stage. No live or management conversation occurs onscene.
- **Pages:** 507020302P1-38

**Account:** The earlier live and Ume's fever-related absence are discussed. Ume/Hina affirm the fever while Ume contests Emoco's excitement-cause taunt; Shinju disputes Ume's explanation of a word. Ume predicts4Uwill overshadow the live. Haru acknowledges4U's strength and her own uncertainty but grounds stage confidence in the group's current ongoing work; Kajika, Sumire and Shinju give their own appraisals. Ume explicitly grants the present group rival status as an exception, and Haru invites shared effort. Hina interprets this as Ume's purpose, which Ume denies.
- **Pages:** 507020302P40-66

**Account:** Haru raises LeSca and her Kajika collaboration as upcoming live units. Hina supplies full はる☆ジカ（ちいさな）, learned by email from Shinju, who confirms transmission while lacking its rationale. Hina reports advice and family contact with Shinju; Kajika confirms a shop visit. Ume is surprised and objects to the rivalry mood being spoiled. Haru asks for Ume's address; Ume resists, Emoco taunts about contact-list contents and both Ume/Haru renew competitive vows. No address is provided.
- **Pages:** 507020302P70-108

**Account:** After departure, the Nanasta four separately articulate renewed motivation and a jointly labelled rally. The4Ucoda gives Hina a positive visit appraisal, Emoco a qualified diversion appraisal and Ume a voiced-text ellipsis. Emoco speculates about address regret; Ume denies it and renews superiority claims. Ume's follow-me instruction elicits an unresolved leadership dispute with Emoco. Hina asks them to stop, the two resume cheek-poking and Hina protests before the end cue.

### Complete-family causal structure

- The summons gives Ume an opportunity to stage a rival encounter but does not distribute her immediate purpose to the whole band. Her desired dramatic response repeatedly fails: bystanders are unsure how to respond, Emoco deflates the performance and Haru welcomes rather than fears the announced appearance. This response structure supports comedy without requiring that every antagonistic claim is secretly false affection.
- Concrete counter-information changes the conversation. A reported day off and cancelled private plans challenge the accidental-work explanation; Shinju later provisionally accepts the story to ask what Ume wants to say. Allowing a face-saving premise is a practical conversational tactic, not certification of that premise.
- The withheld live hint is clarified by Emoco's report of management notice/welcome and Haru's own invitation. This qualifies the rhetoric of intrusion without inventing an observed permission conversation or future performance. The source distinguishes planned audience surprise from necessarily unapproved access.
- Ume's superiority prediction elicits a substantive reply, not only hospitality: Haru concedes limits and uncertainty about idol identity while grounding confidence in the present group's work. Other members retain difficulty and pressure. Ume's explicit exceptional rival acknowledgment follows that reply, so a local relational change is positively supported rather than merely guessed beneath denials.
- The full-name correction reveals an independent Hina/Shinju information path. Her reported email, advice and family contact surprise Ume and complicate a simple4U-versus-Nanasta model. Name transmission does not transfer its rationale, and an existing tie does not complete Haru's separate address request.
- Departure produces two receptions. Nanasta expresses renewed effort, Hina values the visit, Emoco calls it a diversion and Ume's unspoken state is left to other characters' speculation and her denial. Shared motivation does not give every participant the same feeling or erase continuing disagreement.
- The last hierarchy dispute repeats a pattern from the first chapter: Hina's attempt to stop Ume/Emoco's conflict becomes a cheek-poking target. The repetition preserves her protest alongside the comic cooperation, so outward rival recognition and internal warmth cannot be treated as total resolution, universal consent or repair.

### Complete-family epistemic boundaries

- Explicit chapter continuity and resumed live discussion bind the two documents locally. August and Ume's 'this year' remain source-local timing without an absolute year, a calendar bridge from the anniversary/LeSca families or a forced position relative to earlier Christmas/NewYearaccounts.
- Hina and Emoco's ignorance of Ume's immediate park summons is not ignorance of every future4Uplan; Emoco later reports a separate known live arrangement. The first-chapter known-then freeze remains prior to Ume's explicit conditional recognition and the second chapter's contact/name disclosures.
- Emoco reports having informed management and quotes a welcome; Haru independently speaks an actual present welcome. SourceP115何咲コニルis retained literally with its possible but unadjudicated Coney reading. No new identity, concealedNicoleknowledge or exact unseen management exchange is fabricated.
- The earlier appearance, Ume's absence and fever have participants' acknowledgment; the excitement cause, 知恵熱 interpretation and dismissal rhetoric are disputed/taunting claims. No diagnosis, actual firing or stable Ume-less4Ulineup is established.
- Ume explicitly recognizes these present Nanasta members as rivals while preserving a general anti-idol disclaimer and a performance demand. Hina's interpretation of why she came and Emoco's later regret speculation remain attributed and opposed by Ume's denials.
- Full はる☆ジカ（ちいさな）is the existingUNIT0033route, with earlier Christmas-limited/continuation-undecided qualifications retained. Haru's new upcoming-stage framing is a prospective local plan, not proof of permanent formation, a duplicate unit or completed public performance. Hina knows the name from Shinju; Shinju does not know the reason for the qualifier.
- Reported Hina/Shinju emails, advice, sister play and the shop visit support a bounded existing relationship, not new unnamed-sister discovery, romance or complete access to all past messages. Ume's surprised reaction marks a knowledge limit; phonebook-content taunts are not an audit.
- Haru requests Ume's address but receives none in this family. Emoco's regret guess, Hina's later-opportunity suggestion and Ume's rebuttal do not complete the exchange or settle inner motive. Leadership claims remain disputed rather than authoritative organization data.
- Hina explicitly protests poking in both chapters; Sumire's friendly appraisal, comic presentation and later collaborative ambition do not rewrite that dissent as consent. Group speaker labels remain written carriers, not separately heard unanimous agreement.

### Complete-family character relations

- Ume combines status defense, performed rival theatrics, repeated denial and an actual qualified acknowledgment of the group's present work. Her potential wish for contact remains inference/speculation, not a definitive hidden essence. Her leadership claim and cheek-poking participation retain counterevidence to a frictionless affectionate portrait; prior harm/fandom/nonforgiveness remain current.
- Emoco is a clear information-giver about the live while also deflating Ume, making harsh absence/firing/contact-list claims and participating in protested poking. Her qualified enjoyment does not make every assertion fact or erase witness/care/complicity. Current bass self-role and older guitar-language qualification persist without any new performed instrument evidence.
- Hina has her own cancelled tutoring plan, independent Shinju tie and name knowledge, positive reception and repeated dissent. She mediates without being only a mediator; agency and pleasure do not eliminate the cost when the pair turns cooperation against her objections.
- Haru answers4Ustrength with confidence in continuing collective work, acknowledging conceptual/historical uncertainty and practical limits. Her welcome, name appreciation, contact request and renewed motivation are local positive acts, not global forgiveness, an acquired address or guaranteed future stage quality.
- Sumire contributes help, hosting, a remembered uncomfortable visit and host pride. Her provisional accommodations and friendly appraisal are situated statements rather than evidence every grievance is forgotten or everyone consents.
- Shinju's difficulty receiving care, day-off knowledge, productive questioning, correction of a word and qualified name transmission give her distinct agency. Neither a height insult nor current assertiveness reduces her to a child caricature, omniscient judge or pure caretaker.
- Kajika marks her earlier absence, reports difficult rehearsal, anticipates audience pleasure, confirms a shop visit and joins renewed effort. Her collaboration with Haru remains the established qualified route and a new local stage prospect, not a completed permanent contract or independently heard group endorsement.

### Complete-family literary synthesis and rivals

**CL-B0250-054 — Recognition answers present work without settling idol identity**

Haru acknowledges 4U's stage strength, continuing difficulty and uncertainty about idol identity while grounding confidence in the group's ongoing present work. Ume then explicitly recognizes these Nanasta members as worthwhile rivals, retaining a disclaimer and demand. This is a qualified local recognition, stronger than guessing affection beneath denial and narrower than a universal ideological conversion.
